SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties

- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	package com.prahlin.cinerific
     2	
     3	import android.app.UiModeManager
     4	import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
     5	import android.graphics.Color
     6	import android.os.Build
     7	import android.os.Bundle
     8	import android.os.SystemClock
     9	import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
    10	import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
    11	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    12	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge

    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    15	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme
    16	
    17	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    18	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    19	        val bootStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
    20	        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
    21	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    22	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    23	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    24	            )
    25	        }
    26	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    27	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    28	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    29	        )
    30	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    31	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    32	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    34	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    35	        }
    36	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    37	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    38	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    39	        }
    40	        setContent {
    41	            CinerificTheme {
    42	                CinerificApp(bootStartMillis = bootStartMillis)
    43	            }
    44	        }
    45	    }
    46	}

   123	@Composable
   124	fun CinerificApp(bootStartMillis: Long = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) {
   125	    val introBootStartMillis by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(bootStartMillis) }
   126	    var showHome by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   127	    var signedInProfile by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificProfile.Guest) }
   128	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   129	    var introSnapshot by rememberSaveable(stateSaver = CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver) {
   130	        mutableStateOf(CinerificIntroSnapshot())
   131	    }
   132	
   133	    if (showHome) {
   134	        CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   135	            CinerificMainExperience(
   136	                signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   137	                selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   138	                onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   139	                onSignOut = {
   140	                    signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   141	                    introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   142	                    showHome = false
   143	                }
   144	            )
   145	        }
   146	    } else {
   147	        AndroidView(
   148	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   149	            factory = { context ->
   150	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   151	                    this.bootStartMillis = introBootStartMillis
   152	                    onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   153	                        if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   154	                            introSnapshot = snapshot
   155	                        }
   156	                    }
   157	                    restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   158	                    onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   159	                        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   160	                        signedInProfile = profile
   161	                        showHome = true
   162	                    }
   163	                }
   164	            },
   165	            update = { view ->
   166	                view.bootStartMillis = introBootStartMillis
   167	                view.onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   168	                    if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   169	                        introSnapshot = snapshot
   170	                    }
   171	                }
   172	                view.restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   173	                view.onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   174	                    introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   175	                    signedInProfile = profile
   176	                    showHome = true
   177	                }
   178	            }
   179	        )
   180	    }
   181	}

   193	    @DrawableRes val avatarResId: Int,
   194	    @DrawableRes val nameResId: Int
   195	) {
   196	    Steve(R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.steve_name),
   197	    Martin(R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.martin_name),
   198	    Janny(R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.janny_name),
   199	    Guest(R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.guest_name)

   201	
   202	@Composable
   203	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   204	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   205	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   206	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   207	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   208	) {
   209	    var destination by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   210	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   211	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   212	    var userProgramRatings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, Int>()) }
   213	    var catalogRouteDestinationName by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
   214	    var catalogRouteGenreName by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
   215	    var catalogRouteModeName by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
   216	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   217	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   218	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   219	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   220	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   221	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   222	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   223	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   224	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   225	            },
   226	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   227	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   228	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   229	            }
   230	        )
   231	    }
   232	    val catalogRoute = remember(
   233	        catalogRouteDestinationName,
   234	        catalogRouteGenreName,
   235	        catalogRouteModeName
   236	    ) {
   237	        cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   238	            destinationName = catalogRouteDestinationName,
   239	            genreName = catalogRouteGenreName,
   240	            modeName = catalogRouteModeName
   241	        )
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    fun clearCatalogRoute() {
   245	        catalogRouteDestinationName = ""
   246	        catalogRouteGenreName = ""
   247	        catalogRouteModeName = ""
   248	    }
   249	
   250	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   251	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   252	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   253	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   254	        if (
   255	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   256	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   257	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   258	        ) {
   259	            onSignOut()
   260	        }
   261	    }
   262	
   263	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   264	        clearCatalogRoute()
   265	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   266	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   267	    }
   268	
   269	    fun showCatalog(route: CinerificCatalogRoute) {
   270	        catalogRouteDestinationName = route.destination.name
   271	        catalogRouteGenreName = route.genre.name
   272	        catalogRouteModeName = route.mode.name
   273	        destination = route.destination
   274	    }
   275	
   276	    fun rateProgram(title: String, rating: Int) {
   277	        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings + (title to rating.coerceIn(1, 5))
   278	    }
   279	
   280	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   281	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   282	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   283	            return
   284	        }
   285	
   286	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   287	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   288	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   289	            isShow = isShow
   290	        )
   291	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   292	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   293	            return
   294	        }
   295	
   296	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   297	    }
   298	
   299	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   300	        Box(
   301	            modifier = Modifier
   302	                .fillMaxSize()
   303	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   304	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   305	                        while (true) {
   306	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   307	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   308	                        }
   309	                    }
   310	                }
   311	        ) {
   312	            when (destination) {
   313	                CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   314	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   315	                    onCatalogSelected = ::showCatalog,
   316	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   317	                )
   318	                CinerificDestination.Movies,
   319	                CinerificDestination.Shows,
   320	                CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   321	                CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   322	                    destination = destination,
   323	                    signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   324	                    selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   325	                    onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   326	                    autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   327	                    onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   328	                        autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   329	                        lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   330	                    },
   331	                    onSignOut = onSignOut,
   332	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   333	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   334	                    userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   335	                    onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   336	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   337	                    catalogRoute = catalogRoute?.takeIf { it.destination == destination },
   338	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   339	                )
   340	                CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   341	                    programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   342	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   343	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   344	                    userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   345	                    onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   346	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   347	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   348	                )
   349	            }
   350	
   351	            CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   352	                currentDestination = destination,
   353	                onDestinationSelected = {
   354	                    clearCatalogRoute()
   355	                    destination = it
   356	                },
   357	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   358	            )
   359	
   360	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   361	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   362	                currentDestination = destination,
   363	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   364	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   365	            )
   366	        }
   367	    }
   368	}
   369	
   370	private fun cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   371	    destinationName: String,
   372	    genreName: String,
   373	    modeName: String
   374	): CinerificCatalogRoute? {
   375	    if (destinationName.isBlank() || genreName.isBlank() || modeName.isBlank()) return null
   376	
   377	    val destination = CinerificDestination.values().firstOrNull { it.name == destinationName } ?: return null
   378	    val genre = ViewportGenre.values().firstOrNull { it.name == genreName } ?: return null
   379	    val mode = ViewportMode.values().firstOrNull { it.name == modeName } ?: return null
   380	
   381	    return CinerificCatalogRoute(
   382	        destination = destination,
   383	        genre = genre,
   384	        mode = mode
   385	    )
   386	}
   387	
   388	@Composable
   389	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   390	    requestId: Int,
   391	    currentDestination: CinerificDestination,
   392	    suppressed: Boolean,
   393	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   394	) {
   395	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   396	
   397	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   398	        if (suppressed) {
   399	            visible = false
   400	            return@LaunchedEffect
   401	        }
   402	        if (requestId == 0) {
   403	            visible = false
   404	            return@LaunchedEffect
   405	        }
   406	        visible = true
   407	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
   408	        visible = false
   409	    }
   410	
   411	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
   412	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
   413	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   414	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
   415	    )
   416	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
   417	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
   418	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   419	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
   420	    )
   421	
   422	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
   423	
   424	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
   425	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
   426	        val isPortraitDetail = currentDestination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails &&
   427	            maxHeight > maxWidth
   428	        val promptY = if (isPortraitDetail) {
   429	            DETAIL_HERO_LOGO_CENTER_Y - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT / 2f
   430	        } else {
   431	            FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y
   432	        }
   433	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   434	            stageScale = stageScale,
   435	            modifier = Modifier
   436	                .absoluteOffset(
   437	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
   438	                    y = (promptY * stageScale).dp
   439	                )
   440	                .graphicsLayer {
   441	                    this.alpha = alpha
   442	                    scaleX = promptScale
   443	                    scaleY = promptScale
   444	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
   445	                }
   446	        )
   447	    }
   448	}
   449	
   450	@Composable
   451	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   452	    stageScale: Float,
   453	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   454	) {
   455	    Box(
   456	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
   457	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   458	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   459	        )
   460	    ) {
   461	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   462	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
   463	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
   464	            val pointer = Path().apply {
   465	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
   466	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   467	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   468	                close()
   469	            }
   470	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
   471	            drawRoundRect(
   472	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
   473	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
   474	                size = Size(
   475	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
   476	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
   477	                ),
   478	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   479	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   480	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   481	                )
   482	            )
   483	            drawRoundRect(
   484	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   485	                topLeft = Offset(
   486	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
   487	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
   488	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
   489	                ),
   490	                size = Size(
   491	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
   492	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
   493	                ),
   494	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   495	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   496	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   497	                ),
   498	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
   499	            )
   500	        }
   501	        Text(
   502	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
   503	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   504	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   505	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
   506	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   507	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
   508	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   509	            modifier = Modifier
   510	                .absoluteOffset(
   511	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
   512	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
   513	                )
   514	                .requiredSize(
   515	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   516	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   517	                )
   518	        )
   519	    }
   520	}
   521	
   522	@Composable
   523	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
   524	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   525	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
   526	    val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   527	        p,
   528	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   529	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   530	    )
   531	    val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
   532	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
   533	        solidProgress = blackToPurple,
   534	        gradientProgress = purpleToSettle
   535	    )
   536	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   537	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   538	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   539	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   540	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
   541	
   542	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   543	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
   544	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
   545	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
   546	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(size.width > size.height)
   547	
   548	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
   549	
   550	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   551	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   552	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   553	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   554	                end = baseBounds,
   555	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   556	            )
   557	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   558	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   559	                end = baseBounds,
   560	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   561	            )
   562	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   563	
   564	            drawFigmaImage(
   565	                image = images.logoSimple,
   566	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   567	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   568	                stageTop = stageTop,
   569	                stageScale = stageScale,
   570	                alpha = logoAlpha
   571	            )
   572	            drawFigmaImage(
   573	                image = images.logoEyes,
   574	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   575	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   576	                stageTop = stageTop,
   577	                stageScale = stageScale,
   578	                alpha = logoAlpha
   579	            )
   580	        }
   581	
   582	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
   583	            val y = avatarYOffset
   584	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   585	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   586	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   587	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   588	
   589	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   590	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   591	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   592	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   593	        }
   594	    }
   595	}

   604	private fun IntroFrame2FromFigma() {
   605	    // Node 474:7311 with Logo Slide 1 instance 478:6917.
   606	    val slideYOffset = remember { Animatable(-417f) }
   607	
   608	    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
   609	        slideYOffset.snapTo(-417f)
   610	        slideYOffset.animateTo(
   611	            targetValue = 0f,
   612	            animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 1800, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing)
   613	        )
   614	    }

   619	}

   668	            CINERIFIC_LOADING_SPINNER_CANVAS_HEIGHT /

   677	        )
   678	    }

   778	            martinAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test).asImageBitmap(),
   779	            jannyAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test).asImageBitmap(),
   780	            guestAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test).asImageBitmap(),
   781	            steveName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.steve_name).asImageBitmap(),
   782	            martinName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.martin_name).asImageBitmap(),
   783	            jannyName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.janny_name).asImageBitmap(),

   785	        )

   788	
   789	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
   790	    image: ImageBitmap,
   791	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
   792	    stageLeft: Float,
   793	    stageTop: Float,
   794	    stageScale: Float,
   795	    alpha: Float = 1f,
   796	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
   797	) {
   798	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
   799	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
   800	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
   801	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
   802	
   803	    if (clipCircle) {
   804	        val clip = Path().apply {
   805	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
   806	        }
   807	        clipPath(clip) {
   808	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   809	        }
   810	    } else {
   811	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   812	    }
   813	}
   814	
   815	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
   816	    image: ImageBitmap,
   817	    left: Float,
   818	    top: Float,
   819	    width: Float,
   820	    height: Float,
   821	    alpha: Float
   822	) {
   823	    drawImage(
   824	        image = image,
   825	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
   826	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
   827	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   828	    )
   829	}

   831	@Composable
   832	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
   833	    scale: Float,
   834	    entryProgress: Float,
   835	    finalProgress: Float,
   836	    alpha: Float
   837	) {
   838	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   839	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   840	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   841	        end = baseBounds,
   842	        amount = entryProgress
   843	    )
   844	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   845	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   846	        end = baseBounds,
   847	        amount = entryProgress
   848	    )
   849	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   850	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   851	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   852	
   853	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   854	        base = baseBounds,
   855	        target = simpleBounds,
   856	        scale = scale,
   857	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
   858	        alpha = alpha
   859	    )
   860	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   861	        base = baseBounds,
   862	        target = eyesBounds,
   863	        scale = scale,
   864	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
   865	        alpha = alpha
   866	    )
   867	}

   869	@Composable
   870	private fun AvatarSelectionLayer(scale: Float, alpha: Float, yOffset: Float) {
   871	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
   872	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   873	    val isLandscape = configuration.screenWidthDp > configuration.screenHeightDp
   874	    val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(isLandscape)
   875	    val accountPromptExtraShiftY = signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape)
   876	    val accountPromptTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape)
   877	    val accountPromptSecondaryTextSize = accountPromptTextSize * ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SECONDARY_TEXT_SCALE
   878	    Box(
   879	        modifier = Modifier
   880	            .requiredSize(figma(1194f, scale), figma(834f, scale))
   881	            .graphicsLayer {
   882	                this.alpha = alpha
   883	                translationY = yOffset * scale * density
   884	            }
   885	    ) {
   886	        // Figma Logo Slide 2, Property 1=Variant2, constrained inside Sign-in Frame.
   887	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 152f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   888	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 390f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   889	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 628f, y = 454f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   890	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 866f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   891	
   892	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 130f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_name)
   893	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 368f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_name)
   894	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 606f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_name)
   895	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 854f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 200f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_name)
   896	        SignInPromptText(
   897	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
   898	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LEFT_ANCHOR_X,
   899	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   900	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
   901	            textAlign = TextAlign.Left,
   902	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   903	            scale = scale
   904	        )
   905	        SignInPromptText(
   906	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
   907	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH / 2f,
   908	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   909	            textSize = accountPromptTextSize,
   910	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   911	            scale = scale
   912	        )
   913	        SignInPromptText(
   914	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
   915	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_RIGHT_ANCHOR_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH,
   916	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   917	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
   918	            textAlign = TextAlign.Right,
   919	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   920	            scale = scale
   921	        )
   922	    }
   923	}
   924	
   925	@Composable
   926	private fun SignInPromptText(
   927	    text: String,
   928	    x: Float,
   929	    y: Float,
   930	    textSize: Float,
   931	    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
   932	    textAlign: TextAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   933	    scale: Float
   934	) {
   935	    val contentAlignment = when (textAlign) {
   936	        TextAlign.Left -> Alignment.CenterStart
   937	        TextAlign.Right -> Alignment.CenterEnd
   938	        else -> Alignment.Center
   939	    }
   940	    Box(
   941	        modifier = Modifier
   942	            .absoluteOffset(
   943	                x = figma(x, scale),
   944	                y = figma(y, scale)
   945	            )
   946	            .requiredSize(
   947	                width = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH, scale),
   948	                height = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_HEIGHT, scale)
   949	            ),
   950	        contentAlignment = contentAlignment
   951	    ) {
   952	        Text(
   953	            text = text,
   954	            color = Color.White,
   955	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   956	            fontSize = (textSize * scale).sp,
   957	            fontWeight = fontWeight,
   958	            lineHeight = (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LINE_HEIGHT * textSize / ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * scale).sp,
   959	            maxLines = 1,
   960	            softWrap = false,
   961	            textAlign = textAlign
   962	        )
   963	    }
   964	}

   966	@Composable
   967	private fun FigmaAvatarImage(
   968	    x: Float,
   969	    y: Float,
   970	    w: Float,
   971	    h: Float,
   972	    scale: Float,
   973	    @DrawableRes resId: Int
   974	) {
   975	    Image(
   976	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
   977	        contentDescription = null,
   978	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
   979	        modifier = Modifier
   980	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
   981	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
   982	            .clip(CircleShape)
   983	    )
   984	}

   986	@Composable
   987	private fun LogoDot(x: Float, y: Float, scale: Float, active: Boolean) {
   988	    Box(
   989	        modifier = Modifier
   990	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
   991	            .requiredSize(figma(50.4f, scale), figma(50.4f, scale))

   993	            .background(if (active) Color(0xFFE7E7E7) else Color(0xFF050505))
   994	    )
   995	}

   997	@Composable
   998	private fun PromoBackgroundLayer(
   999	    scale: Float,
  1000	    darkOverlay: Float,
  1001	    @DrawableRes backgroundResId: Int,
  1002	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1003	) {
  1004	    // Nodes 793:7049 / 793:7050 at x:0 y:0 size:1584x1584.
  1005	    FigmaAssetImage(
  1006	        x = 0f,
  1007	        y = 0f,
  1008	        w = 1584f,
  1009	        h = 1584f,
  1010	        scale = scale,
  1011	        resId = backgroundResId,
  1012	        alpha = alpha
  1013	    )
  1014	
  1015	    if (darkOverlay > 0f) {
  1016	        Box(
  1017	            modifier = Modifier
  1018	                .absoluteOffset(x = figma(0f, scale), y = figma(0f, scale))
  1019	                .requiredSize(figma(1584f, scale), figma(1584f, scale))
  1020	                .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1021	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = darkOverlay))
  1022	        )
  1023	    }
  1024	}
  1025	
  1026	@Composable
  1027	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1028	    base: FigmaBounds,
  1029	    target: FigmaBounds,
  1030	    scale: Float,
  1031	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1032	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1033	) {
  1034	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1035	    Image(
  1036	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1037	        contentDescription = null,
  1038	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1039	        modifier = Modifier
  1040	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
  1041	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
  1042	            .graphicsLayer {
  1043	                this.alpha = alpha
  1044	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
  1045	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
  1046	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
  1047	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
  1048	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
  1049	            }
  1050	    )
  1051	}
  1052	
  1053	@Composable
  1054	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
  1055	    x: Float,
  1056	    y: Float,
  1057	    w: Float,
  1058	    h: Float,
  1059	    scale: Float,
  1060	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1061	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1062	) {
  1063	    Image(
  1064	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1065	        contentDescription = null,
  1066	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1067	        modifier = Modifier
  1068	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1069	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1070	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1071	    )
  1072	}
  1073	
  1074	@Composable
  1075	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
  1076	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1077	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
  1078	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
  1079	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1080	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
  1081	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
  1082	
  1083	        Box(
  1084	            modifier = Modifier
  1085	                .fillMaxSize()
  1086	                .background(background)
  1087	        ) {
  1088	            BoxWithConstraints(
  1089	                modifier = Modifier
  1090	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
  1091	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
  1092	            ) {
  1093	                content(scale)
  1094	            }
  1095	        }
  1096	    }
  1097	}
  1098	
  1099	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
  1100	
  1101	private fun signInStackShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1102	    return if (isLandscape) {
  1103	        SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1104	    } else {
  1105	        SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1106	    }
  1107	}
  1108	
  1109	private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1110	    return if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1111	}
  1112	
  1113	private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1114	    return if (isLandscape) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  1115	}
  1116	
  1117	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
  1118	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
  1119	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
  1120	        colors = listOf(
  1121	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
  1122	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
  1123	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
  1124	        )
  1125	    )
  1126	}
  1127	
  1128	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
  1129	    colors = listOf(color, color)
  1130	)
  1131	
  1132	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1133	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1134	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1135	}
  1136	
  1137	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
  1138	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1139	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
  1140	}
  1141	
  1142	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1143	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1144	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1145	}
  1146	
  1147	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1148	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1149	}
  1150	
  1151	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1152	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1153	}
  1154	
  1155	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1156	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1157	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1158	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1159	    return base + bounce
  1160	}
  1161	
  1162	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1163	
  1164	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
  1165	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1166	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1167	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1168	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1169	)
  1170	
  1171	private val CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver = listSaver<CinerificIntroSnapshot, Any>(
  1172	    save = { snapshot ->
  1173	        listOf(
  1174	            snapshot.activeFlowName,
  1175	            snapshot.usernameText,
  1176	            snapshot.passwordText,
  1177	            snapshot.confirmPasswordText,
  1178	            snapshot.emailText,
  1179	            snapshot.monthText,
  1180	            snapshot.dayText,
  1181	            snapshot.yearText,
  1182	            snapshot.subscriptionTierText,
  1183	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName,
  1184	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName,
  1185	            snapshot.rememberMeChecked,
  1186	            snapshot.createAvatarIndex
  1187	        )
  1188	    },
  1189	    restore = { values ->
  1190	        CinerificIntroSnapshot(
  1191	            activeFlowName = values.getOrNull(0) as? String ?: "",
  1192	            usernameText = values.getOrNull(1) as? String ?: "",
  1193	            passwordText = values.getOrNull(2) as? String ?: "",
  1194	            confirmPasswordText = values.getOrNull(3) as? String ?: "",
  1195	            emailText = values.getOrNull(4) as? String ?: "",
  1196	            monthText = values.getOrNull(5) as? String ?: "Mon",
  1197	            dayText = values.getOrNull(6) as? String ?: "Day",
  1198	            yearText = values.getOrNull(7) as? String ?: "Year",
  1199	            subscriptionTierText = values.getOrNull(8) as? String ?: "Subscription Tier",
  1200	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = values.getOrNull(9) as? String ?: "Password",
  1201	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = values.getOrNull(10) as? String ?: "Idle",
  1202	            rememberMeChecked = values.getOrNull(11) as? Boolean ?: false,
  1203	            createAvatarIndex = values.getOrNull(12) as? Int ?: 0
  1204	        )
  1205	    }
  1206	)

   403	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   404	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   405	
   406	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   407	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   408	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   409	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   410	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   411	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   412	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   413	
   414	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   415	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   416	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   417	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   418	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   419	        pressedMockField = null
   420	        pressedRememberMe = false
   421	        pressedBackChevron = false
   422	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   423	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   424	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   425	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   426	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   427	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   428	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   429	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   430	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   431	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   432	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   433	        activeComposingText = ""
   434	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   435	        focusedMockField = null
   436	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   437	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   438	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   439	
   440	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   441	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   442	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   443	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   444	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   445	        } else {
   446	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   447	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   448	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   449	        }
   450	
   451	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   452	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   453	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   454	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   455	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   456	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   457	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   458	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   459	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   460	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   461	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   462	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   463	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   464	        ) {
   465	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   466	        } else {
   467	            null
   468	        }
   469	
   470	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   471	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   472	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   473	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   474	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   475	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   476	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   477	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   478	        }
   479	
   480	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   481	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   482	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   483	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   484	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   485	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   486	    }
   487	
   488	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   489	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   490	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   491	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   492	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   493	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   494	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   495	        pressedMockField = null
   496	        pressedRememberMe = false
   497	        pressedBackChevron = false
   498	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   499	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   500	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   501	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   502	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   503	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   504	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   505	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   506	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   507	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   508	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   509	    }

   512	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   513	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
