   515	
   516	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   517	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   518	
   519	        val progress = bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis)
   520	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   521	            progress,
   522	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   523	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   524	        )
   525	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   526	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   527	
   528	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   529	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   530	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   531	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   532	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   533	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   534	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   535	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   536	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   537	
   538	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   539	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   540	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha, formFocusMotion)
   541	        }
   542	
   543	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   544	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   545	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   546	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   547	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   548	            val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
   549	            if (stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   550	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   551	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   552	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   553	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   554	
   555	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   556	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   557	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   558	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   559	                drawAccountPrompt(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, y, stackAlpha)
   560	            }
   561	        }
   562	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   563	            drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   564	        }
   565	
   566	        if (
   567	            progress < 1f ||
   568	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   569	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   570	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   571	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   572	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   573	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   574	            isMockInputDimAnimating()
   575	        ) {
   576	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   577	        }
   578	    }
   579	
   580	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   581	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   582	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   583	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   584	                    mockTouchDownX = event.x
   585	                    mockTouchDownY = event.y
   586	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   587	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   588	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   589	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   590	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   591	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   592	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   593	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   594	                    } else {
   595	                        null
   596	                    }
   597	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   598	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   599	                    } else {
   600	                        null
   601	                    }
   602	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   603	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   604	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   605	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   606	                    ) {
   607	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   608	                    } else {
   609	                        null
   610	                    }
   611	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   612	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   613	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   614	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   615	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   616	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   617	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   618	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   619	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   620	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   621	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   622	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   623	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   624	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   625	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   626	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   627	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   628	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   629	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   630	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   631	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   632	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   633	                    ) {
   634	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   635	                    } else {
   636	                        null
   637	                    }
   638	                    pressedMockField = if (
   639	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   640	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   641	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   642	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   643	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   644	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   645	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   646	                    ) {
   647	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   648	                    } else {
   649	                        null
   650	                    }
   651	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   652	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   653	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   654	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   655	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   656	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   657	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   658	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   659	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   660	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   661	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   662	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   663	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   664	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   665	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   666	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   667	                    return true
   668	                }
   669	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   670	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   671	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   672	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   673	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   674	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   675	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   676	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   677	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   678	                ) {
   679	                    null
   680	                } else {
   681	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   682	                }
   683	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   684	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   685	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   686	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   687	            }
   688	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   689	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   690	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   691	                        return true
   692	                    }
   693	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   694	                        return true
   695	                    }
   696	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   697	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   698	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   699	                    }
   700	                    return true
   701	                }
   702	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   703	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   704	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   705	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   706	            }
   707	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   708	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   709	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   710	                    pressedMockField = null
   711	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   712	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   713	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   714	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   715	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   716	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   717	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   718	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   719	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   720	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   721	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   722	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   723	                    return true
   724	                }
   725	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   726	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   727	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   728	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   729	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   730	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   731	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   732	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   733	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   734	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   735	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   736	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   737	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   738	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   739	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   740	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   741	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   742	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   743	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   744	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   745	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   746	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   747	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   748	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   749	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   750	                        pressedOption != null &&
   751	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
   752	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
   753	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
   754	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
   755	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
   756	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
   757	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
   758	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
   759	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
   760	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
   761	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   762	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
   763	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
   764	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
   765	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
   766	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
   767	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
   768	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
   769	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
   770	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   771	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   772	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
   773	                        pressedOption == null &&
   774	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   775	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
   776	                    pressedMockField = null
   777	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   778	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   779	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   780	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   781	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   782	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   783	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   784	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   785	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   786	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   787	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   788	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   789	                    super.performClick()
   790	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
   791	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   792	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
   793	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   794	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   795	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
   796	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
   797	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   798	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   799	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
   800	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
   801	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   802	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
   803	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
   804	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   805	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   806	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   807	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   808	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   809	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
   810	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
   811	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
   812	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   813	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   814	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   815	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   816	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
   817	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
   818	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
   819	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   820	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
   821	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
   822	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
   823	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
   824	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
   825	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   826	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   827	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   828	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
   829	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
   830	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   831	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
   832	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
   833	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
   834	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   835	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   836	                        }
   837	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   838	                    } else {
   839	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   840	                    }
   841	                    return true
   842	                }
   843	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   844	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   845	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   846	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
   847	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   848	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   849	                val shouldNavigate = pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
   850	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
   851	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   852	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   853	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   854	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   855	                if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
   856	                    super.performClick()
   857	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
   858	                    true
   859	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
   860	                    super.performClick()
   861	                    openMockSignInScreen()
   862	                    true
   863	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
   864	                    super.performClick()
   865	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
   866	                    true
   867	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
   868	                    performClick()
   869	                    true
   870	                } else {
   871	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
   872	                    false
   873	                }
   874	            }
   875	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
   876	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   877	                clickAvatarProfile = null
   878	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   879	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   880	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   881	                pressedMockField = null
   882	                pressedRememberMe = false
   883	                pressedBackChevron = false
   884	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   885	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   886	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   887	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   888	                pressedMockDropdown = null
   889	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   890	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   891	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   892	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   893	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   894	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   895	                false
   896	            }
   897	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   898	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
   899	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   900	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
   901	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
   902	        }
   903	    }
   904	
   905	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
   906	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
   907	    }
   908	
   909	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
   910	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
   911	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
   912	            MockSignInField.Password,
   913	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
   914	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
   915	            MockSignInField.Month,
   916	            MockSignInField.Day,
   917	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
   918	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
   919	        }
   920	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
   921	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
   922	        } else {
   923	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
   924	        }
   925	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
   926	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
   927	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
   928	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
   929	    }
   930	
   931	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
   932	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
   933	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
   934	        }
   935	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
   936	    }
   937	
   938	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
   939	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
   940	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
   941	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
   942	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
   943	        }
   944	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
   945	    }
   946	
   947	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
   948	        super.performClick()
   949	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
   950	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
   951	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
   952	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   953	        return true
   954	    }
   955	
   956	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
   957	        canvas: Canvas,
   958	        stageLeft: Float,
   959	        stageTop: Float,
   960	        stageScale: Float,
   961	        progress: Float,
   962	        alpha: Float,
   963	        formFocusMotion: Float
   964	    ) {
   965	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   966	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   967	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
   968	        val base = offsetBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), focusShiftY)
   969	        val final = offsetBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), focusShiftY)
   970	
   971	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
   972	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   973	            return
   974	        }
   975	
   976	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
   977	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
   978	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   979	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   980	    }
   981	
   982	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
   983	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
   984	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
   985	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
   986	            return
   987	        }
   988	
   989	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
   990	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
   991	            0f,
   992	            0f,
   993	            0f,
   994	            height.toFloat(),
   995	            intArrayOf(
   996	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
   997	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
   998	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
   999	            ),
  1000	            null,
  1001	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1002	        )
  1003	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1004	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1005	
  1006	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1007	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1008	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1009	        }
  1010	    }
  1011	
  1012	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1013	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1014	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1015	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1016	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1017	    }
  1018	
  1019	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1020	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1021	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1022	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1023	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1024	    }
  1025	
  1026	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1027	        canvas: Canvas,
  1028	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1029	        bounds: Bounds,
  1030	        stageLeft: Float,
  1031	        stageTop: Float,
  1032	        stageScale: Float,
  1033	        alpha: Float
  1034	    ) {
  1035	        tempRect.set(
  1036	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1037	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1038	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1039	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1040	        )
  1041	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1042	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1043	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1044	    }
  1045	
  1046	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1047	        canvas: Canvas,
  1048	        stageLeft: Float,
  1049	        stageTop: Float,
  1050	        stageScale: Float,
  1051	        yOffset: Float,
  1052	        alpha: Float
  1053	    ) {
  1054	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1055	        val textSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1056	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1057	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1058	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1059	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1060	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1061	
  1062	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1063	        val centerY = stageTop +
  1064	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1065	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1066	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1067	        val secondaryBaselineY = centerY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1068	        val primaryBaselineY = centerY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1069	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1070	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1071	            canvas.drawText(
  1072	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1073	                stageLeft + accountPromptCreateCenterX() * stageScale,
  1074	                secondaryBaselineY,
  1075	                accountPromptPaint
  1076	            )
  1077	        }
  1078	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn) {
  1079	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1080	            canvas.drawText(
  1081	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1082	                stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale,
  1083	                primaryBaselineY,
  1084	                formTitlePaint
  1085	            )
  1086	        }
  1087	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1088	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1089	            canvas.drawText(
  1090	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1091	                stageLeft + accountPromptForgotCenterX() * stageScale,
  1092	                secondaryBaselineY,
  1093	                accountPromptPaint
  1094	            )
  1095	        }
  1096	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1097	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1098	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1099	    }
  1100	
  1101	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1102	        canvas: Canvas,
  1103	        stageLeft: Float,
  1104	        stageTop: Float,
  1105	        stageScale: Float,
  1106	        progress: Float,
  1107	        formFocusMotion: Float
  1108	    ) {
  1109	        updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1110	
  1111	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1112	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1113	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1114	            return
  1115	        }
  1116	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1117	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1118	            return
  1119	        }
  1120	
  1121	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1122	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1123	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1124	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1125	        val usernameY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset
  1126	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP
  1127	
  1128	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1129	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1130	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1131	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1132	
  1133	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1134	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1135	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1136	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1137	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1138	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1139	            canvas,
  1140	            fieldX,
  1141	            rememberMeY,
  1142	            stageLeft,
  1143	            stageTop,
  1144	            stageScale,
  1145	            alpha
  1146	        )
  1147	        drawBackChevron(
  1148	            canvas,
  1149	            fieldX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f,
  1150	            rememberMeY + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_TOP_GAP,
  1151	            stageLeft,
  1152	            stageTop,
  1153	            stageScale,
  1154	            alpha
  1155	        )
  1156	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1157	            canvas,
  1158	            stageLeft,
  1159	            stageTop,
  1160	            stageScale,
  1161	            alpha,
  1162	            yOffset,
  1163	            focusShiftY

  1369	
  1370	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1371	        canvas: Canvas,
  1372	        stageLeft: Float,
  1373	        stageTop: Float,
  1374	        stageScale: Float,
  1375	        alpha: Float,
  1376	        yOffset: Float,
  1377	        focusShiftY: Float
  1378	    ) {
  1379	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1380	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1381	
  1382	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1383	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1384	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1385	
  1386	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1387	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1388	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1389	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1390	
  1391	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1392	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1393	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1394	                canvas,
  1395	                activeDropdown,
  1396	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1397	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1398	                stageLeft,
  1399	                stageTop,
  1400	                stageScale,
  1401	                alpha,
  1402	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1403	            )
  1404	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1405	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1406	            }
  1407	            return
  1408	        }
  1409	
  1410	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1411	        drawMockSignInField(
  1412	            canvas,
  1413	            activeField,
  1414	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1415	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1416	            stageLeft,
  1417	            stageTop,
  1418	            stageScale,
  1419	            alpha,
  1420	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1421	        )
  1422	    }

  1424	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  1425	        canvas: Canvas,
  1426	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1427	        stageLeft: Float,
  1428	        stageTop: Float,
  1429	        stageScale: Float,
  1430	        alpha: Float
  1431	    ) {
  1432	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1433	        val sourceTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1434	        val targetTextSize = MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1435	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1436	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1437	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop +
  1438	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stageScale
  1439	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1440	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1441	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1442	        val targetBaselineY = stageTop + (fieldTopY - MOCK_FORM_TITLE_BASELINE_GAP) * stageScale
  1443	
  1444	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  1445	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1446	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1447	        canvas.drawText(
  1448	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1449	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  1450	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  1451	            formTitlePaint
  1452	        )
  1453	    }

  1516	
  1517	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  1518	        canvas: Canvas,
  1519	        x: Float,
  1520	        y: Float,
  1521	        stageLeft: Float,
  1522	        stageTop: Float,
  1523	        stageScale: Float,
  1524	        alpha: Float,
  1525	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1526	    ) {
  1527	        tempRect.set(
  1528	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1529	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1530	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  1531	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1532	        )
  1533	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  1534	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  1535	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  1536	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  1537	        val fillAlpha = when {
  1538	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  1539	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  1540	            pressed -> 92f
  1541	            enabled -> 68f
  1542	            else -> 18f
  1543	        }
  1544	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  1545	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  1546	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  1547	            pressed -> 238f
  1548	            enabled -> 210f
  1549	            else -> 118f
  1550	        }
  1551	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1552	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1553	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1554	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1555	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1556	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1557	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  1558	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  1559	        }
  1560	
  1561	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1562	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1563	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1564	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  1565	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  1566	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  1567	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  1568	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  1569	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  1570	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  1571	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  1572	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  1573	            formTitlePaint
  1574	        )
  1575	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  1576	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  1577	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  1578	            enabled -> 255f
  1579	            else -> 138f
  1580	        }).roundToInt()
  1581	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1582	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  1583	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  1584	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  1585	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  1586	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  1587	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  1588	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1589	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  1590	            canvas.drawLine(
  1591	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  1592	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  1593	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1594	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1595	                backChevronPaint
  1596	            )
  1597	            canvas.drawLine(
  1598	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1599	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1600	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  1601	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  1602	                backChevronPaint
  1603	            )
  1604	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  1605	        } else {
  1606	            tempRect.centerX()
  1607	        }
  1608	        canvas.drawText(
  1609	            buttonText,
  1610	            textX,
  1611	            baselineY,
  1612	            formTitlePaint
  1613	        )
  1614	    }
  1615	
  1616	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  1617	        canvas: Canvas,
  1618	        buttonRect: RectF,
  1619	        radius: Float,
  1620	        alpha: Float
  1621	    ) {
  1622	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  1623	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  1624	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  1625	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  1626	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  1627	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1628	            sweepLeft,
  1629	            buttonRect.top,
  1630	            sweepRight,
  1631	            buttonRect.top,
  1632	            intArrayOf(
  1633	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  1634	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  1635	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  1636	            ),
  1637	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  1638	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1639	        )
  1640	        canvas.save()
  1641	        tempPath.reset()
  1642	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  1643	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  1644	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  1645	        canvas.restore()
  1646	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1647	    }
  1648	
  1649	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoverySelector(
  1650	        canvas: Canvas,
  1651	        x: Float,
  1652	        y: Float,
  1653	        stageLeft: Float,
  1654	        stageTop: Float,
  1655	        stageScale: Float,
  1656	        alpha: Float,
  1657	        formWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1658	    ) {
  1659	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1660	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1661	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1662	
  1663	        val labelWidth = formLabelPaint.measureText(MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_TEXT)
  1664	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1665	        val optionBoxWidth = mockForgotRecoverySelectorBoxWidth(
  1666	            usernameTextWidth = formInputPaint.measureText(MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Username.displayText),
  1667	            passwordTextWidth = formInputPaint.measureText(MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password.displayText),
  1668	            baseBoxWidth = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH * stageScale
  1669	        )
  1670	        val layout = mockForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  1671	            left = stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1672	            width = formWidth * stageScale,
  1673	            labelWidth = labelWidth,
  1674	            boxWidth = optionBoxWidth
  1675	        )
  1676	        val rowTop = stageTop + y * stageScale
  1677	        val rowCenterY = rowTop + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  1678	        val labelMetrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  1679	        canvas.drawText(
  1680	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_TEXT,
  1681	            layout.labelX,
  1682	            rowCenterY - (labelMetrics.ascent + labelMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  1683	            formLabelPaint
  1684	        )
  1685	
  1686	        drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  1687	            canvas,

  1689	            layout.usernameBoxX,
  1690	            rowTop,
  1691	            layout.boxWidth,
  1692	            stageScale,
  1693	            alpha
  1694	        )
  1695	        drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(

  1706	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  1707	        canvas: Canvas,
  1708	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  1709	        left: Float,
  1710	        top: Float,
  1711	        boxWidth: Float,
  1712	        stageScale: Float,
  1713	        alpha: Float
  1714	    ) {
  1715	        val right = left + boxWidth
  1716	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  1717	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  1718	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1719	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1720	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1721	            pressed -> 82f
  1722	            selected -> 64f
  1723	            else -> 14f
  1724	        }).roundToInt()
  1725	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1726	            pressed -> 245f
  1727	            selected -> 235f
  1728	            else -> 142f
  1729	        }).roundToInt()
  1730	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  1731	
  1732	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1733	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1734	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1735	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1736	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1737	
  1738	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1739	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  1740	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1741	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1742	        canvas.drawText(
  1743	            target.displayText,
  1744	            tempRect.centerX(),
  1745	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  1746	            formInputPaint
  1747	        )
  1748	    }
  1749	
  1750	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  1751	        canvas: Canvas,
  1752	        text: String,
  1753	        centerX: Float,
  1754	        topY: Float,
  1755	        maxWidth: Float,
  1756	        stageLeft: Float,
  1757	        stageTop: Float,
  1758	        stageScale: Float,
  1759	        alpha: Float
  1760	    ): Float {
  1761	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  1762	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  1763	            canvas,
  1764	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  1765	            centerX,
  1766	            topY,
  1767	            maxWidth,
  1768	            stageLeft,
  1769	            stageTop,
  1770	            stageScale,
  1771	            alpha,
  1772	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  1773	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  1774	        )
  1775	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  1776	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  1777	            canvas,
  1778	            middleText,
  1779	            centerX,
  1780	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1781	            maxWidth,
  1782	            stageLeft,
  1783	            stageTop,
  1784	            stageScale,
  1785	            alpha,
  1786	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  1787	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1788	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  1789	        )
  1790	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  1791	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  1792	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  1793	            canvas,
  1794	            bottomText,
  1795	            centerX,
  1796	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1797	            maxWidth,
  1798	            stageLeft,
  1799	            stageTop,
  1800	            stageScale,
  1801	            alpha,
  1802	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  1803	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1804	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  1805	        )
  1806	    }
  1807	
  1808	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  1809	        canvas: Canvas,
  1810	        text: String,
  1811	        centerX: Float,
  1812	        topY: Float,
  1813	        maxWidth: Float,
  1814	        stageLeft: Float,
  1815	        stageTop: Float,
  1816	        stageScale: Float,
  1817	        alpha: Float,
  1818	        textSize: Float,
  1819	        lineHeight: Float,
  1820	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1821	    ): Float {
  1822	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  1823	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  1824	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  1825	
  1826	        val lines = wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  1827	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1828	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  1829	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  1830	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  1831	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  1832	        }
  1833	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  1834	        lines.forEach { line ->
  1835	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  1836	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  1837	        }
  1838	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  1839	    }

  1841	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  1842	        canvas: Canvas,
  1843	        columnX: Float,
  1844	        firstFieldY: Float,
  1845	        stageLeft: Float,
  1846	        stageTop: Float,
  1847	        stageScale: Float,
  1848	        alpha: Float
  1849	    ) {
  1850	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  1851	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  1852	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  1853	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  1854	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  1855	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  1856	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * avatarScale * stageScale
  1857	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1858	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1859	        canvas.drawText(
  1860	            MOCK_FORM_AVATAR_TEXT,
  1861	            centerX,
  1862	            baselineY,
  1863	            formLabelPaint
  1864	        )
  1865	
  1866	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  1867	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  1868	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  1869	            avatarLeftX,
  1870	            avatarTopY,
  1871	            scaledAvatarSize,
  1872	            scaledAvatarSize
  1873	        )
  1874	        val carouselProgress = mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  1875	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1876	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize)
  1877	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  1878	        val dragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  1879	
  1880	        drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1881	
  1882	        canvas.save()
  1883	        clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1884	        if (
  1885	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  1886	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  1887	            dragDirection != null &&
  1888	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  1889	        ) {
  1890	            val dragSlideDirection = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1891	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1892	                canvas,
  1893	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  1894	                avatarBounds,
  1895	                -dragSlideDirection * dragProgress * slideDistance,
  1896	                stageLeft,
  1897	                stageTop,
  1898	                stageScale,
  1899	                alpha
  1900	            )
  1901	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1902	                canvas,
  1903	                mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1904	                avatarBounds,
  1905	                dragSlideDirection * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance,
  1906	                stageLeft,
  1907	                stageTop,
  1908	                stageScale,
  1909	                alpha
  1910	            )
  1911	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  1912	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1913	                canvas,
  1914	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex,
  1915	                avatarBounds,
  1916	                -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance,
  1917	                stageLeft,
  1918	                stageTop,
  1919	                stageScale,
  1920	                alpha
  1921	            )
  1922	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1923	                canvas,
  1924	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex,
  1925	                avatarBounds,
  1926	                slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance,
  1927	                stageLeft,
  1928	                stageTop,
  1929	                stageScale,
  1930	                alpha
  1931	            )
  1932	        } else {
  1933	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1934	                canvas,
  1935	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  1936	                avatarBounds,
  1937	                0f,
  1938	                stageLeft,
  1939	                stageTop,
  1940	                stageScale,
  1941	                alpha
  1942	            )
  1943	        }
  1944	        canvas.restore()
  1945	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1946	            canvas,
  1947	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  1948	            avatarBounds,
  1949	            stageLeft,
  1950	            stageTop,
  1951	            stageScale,
  1952	            alpha
  1953	        )
  1954	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1955	            canvas,
  1956	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  1957	            avatarBounds,
  1958	            stageLeft,
  1959	            stageTop,
  1960	            stageScale,
  1961	            alpha
  1962	        )
  1963	    }

  2005	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(
  2006	        canvas: Canvas,
  2007	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2008	        stageLeft: Float,
  2009	        stageTop: Float,
  2010	        stageScale: Float
  2011	    ) {
  2012	        tempPath.reset()
  2013	        tempPath.addCircle(
  2014	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  2015	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  2016	            mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds, stageScale),
  2017	            Path.Direction.CW
  2018	        )
  2019	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2020	    }
  2021	
  2022	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds: Bounds, stageScale: Float): Float {
  2023	        return (avatarBounds.w / 2f - avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO) * stageScale
  2024	    }
  2025	
  2026	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2027	        canvas: Canvas,
  2028	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2029	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2030	        stageLeft: Float,
  2031	        stageTop: Float,
  2032	        stageScale: Float,
  2033	        alpha: Float
  2034	    ) {
  2035	        val centerX = stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2036	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2037	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2038	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2039	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2040	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2041	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2042	
  2043	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2044	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2045	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2046	        } else {
  2047	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2048	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2049	        }
  2050	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2051	    }

  2053	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  2054	        canvas: Canvas,
  2055	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2056	        x: Float,
  2057	        y: Float,
  2058	        stageLeft: Float,
  2059	        stageTop: Float,
  2060	        stageScale: Float,
  2061	        alpha: Float,
  2062	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2063	    ) {
  2064	        tempRect.set(
  2065	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2066	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2067	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  2068	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2069	        )
  2070	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2071	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2072	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2073	
  2074	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2075	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2076	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2077	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2078	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2079	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2080	        val displayText = mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown)
  2081	        val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(displayText)
  2082	            ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2083	        val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2084	            (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2085	        } else {
  2086	            0f
  2087	        }
  2088	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - iconLaneWidth - chevronInset * 2f)
  2089	            .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2090	        if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2091	            drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2092	                canvas = canvas,
  2093	                bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2094	                left = tempRect.left + textInset,
  2095	                centerY = tempRect.centerY(),
  2096	                iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2097	                alpha = textAlpha
  2098	            )
  2099	        }
  2100	        canvas.drawText(
  2101	            trailingFittingText(displayText, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2102	            tempRect.left + textInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2103	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  2104	            formInputPaint
  2105	        )
  2106	
  2107	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  2108	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  2109	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  2110	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2111	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  2112	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2113	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2114	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2115	    }

  2117	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  2118	        canvas: Canvas,
  2119	        stageLeft: Float,
  2120	        stageTop: Float,
  2121	        stageScale: Float,
  2122	        alpha: Float
  2123	    ) {
  2124	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  2125	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  2126	
  2127	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2128	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  2129	            dropdown,
  2130	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  2131	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2132	        )
  2133	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2134	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2135	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  2136	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  2137	        val optionAlpha = 255
  2138	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2139	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2140	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  2141	        } else {
  2142	            0f
  2143	        }
  2144	
  2145	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  2146	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  2147	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  2148	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  2149	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  2150	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  2151	            } else {
  2152	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  2153	            }
  2154	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  2155	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  2156	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2157	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  2158	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  2159	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2160	
  2161	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2162	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  2163	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2164	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2165	            val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(option)
  2166	                ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2167	            val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2168	                (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2169	            } else {
  2170	                0f
  2171	            }
  2172	            if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2173	                drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2174	                    canvas = canvas,
  2175	                    bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2176	                    left = left + optionTextInset,
  2177	                    centerY = (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f,
  2178	                    iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2179	                    alpha = optionAlpha
  2180	                )
  2181	            }
  2182	            val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - iconLaneWidth - scrollbarReservedWidth)
  2183	                .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2184	            canvas.drawText(
  2185	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2186	                left + optionTextInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2187	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2188	                formInputPaint
  2189	            )
  2190	        }
  2191	
  2192	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2193	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2194	                canvas,
  2195	                dropdown,
  2196	                top,
  2197	                right,
  2198	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  2199	                stageScale,
  2200	                optionAlpha
  2201	            )
  2202	        }
  2203	    }
  2204	
  2205	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2206	        canvas: Canvas,
  2207	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  2208	        left: Float,
  2209	        centerY: Float,
  2210	        iconSize: Float,
  2211	        alpha: Int
  2212	    ) {
  2213	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  2214	        val width: Float
  2215	        val height: Float
  2216	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  2217	            width = iconSize
  2218	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  2219	        } else {
  2220	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  2221	            height = iconSize
  2222	        }
  2223	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  2224	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  2225	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  2226	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  2227	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  2228	    }
  2229	
  2230	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2231	        canvas: Canvas,
  2232	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2233	        top: Float,
  2234	        right: Float,
  2235	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  2236	        stageScale: Float,
  2237	        alpha: Int
  2238	    ) {
  2239	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  2240	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  2241	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  2242	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  2243	
  2244	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  2245	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  2246	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2247	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2248	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2249	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  2250	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  2251	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  2252	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  2253	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2254	
  2255	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  2256	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2257	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2258	
  2259	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  2260	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2261	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2262	    }

  2264	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  2265	        canvas: Canvas,
  2266	        field: MockSignInField,
  2267	        x: Float,
  2268	        y: Float,
  2269	        stageLeft: Float,
  2270	        stageTop: Float,
  2271	        stageScale: Float,
  2272	        alpha: Float,
  2273	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2274	    ) {
  2275	        tempRect.set(
  2276	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2277	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2278	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  2279	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2280	        )
  2281	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2282	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2283	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2284	
  2285	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  2286	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  2287	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2288	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  2289	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  2290	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  2291	            labelProgress
  2292	        ) * stageScale
  2293	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2294	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2295	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  2296	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2297	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2298	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  2299	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2300	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2301	            labelProgress
  2302	        )
  2303	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  2304	        canvas.drawText(
  2305	            label,
  2306	            labelX,
  2307	            labelBaselineY,
  2308	            formLabelPaint
  2309	        )
  2310	
  2311	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  2312	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  2313	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  2314	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  2315	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2316	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2317	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2318	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2319	
  2320	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  2321	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  2322	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2323	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  2324	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  2325	
  2326	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  2327	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  2328	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2329	                canvas.drawLine(
  2330	                    caretX,
  2331	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  2332	                    caretX,
  2333	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  2334	                    formCaretPaint
  2335	                )
  2336	            }
  2337	        }
  2338	    }
