  2340	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  2341	        canvas: Canvas,
  2342	        x: Float,
  2343	        y: Float,
  2344	        stageLeft: Float,
  2345	        stageTop: Float,
  2346	        stageScale: Float,
  2347	        alpha: Float
  2348	    ) {
  2349	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2350	        val boxLeft = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2351	        val boxTop = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2352	        val boxRight = boxLeft + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2353	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2354	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  2355	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()
  2356	
  2357	        rememberMeBoxFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2358	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2359	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.7f * stageScale
  2360	        tempRect.set(boxLeft, boxTop, boxRight, boxBottom)
  2361	        val radius = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_RADIUS * stageScale
  2362	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxFillPaint)
  2363	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)
  2364	
  2365	        if (rememberMeChecked) {
  2366	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2367	            rememberMeCheckPaint.strokeWidth = 2.45f * stageScale
  2368	            canvas.drawLine(
  2369	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,
  2370	                boxTop + 11.8f * stageScale,
  2371	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2372	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2373	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2374	            )
  2375	            canvas.drawLine(
  2376	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2377	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2378	                boxLeft + 17.2f * stageScale,
  2379	                boxTop + 6.9f * stageScale,
  2380	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2381	            )
  2382	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  2383	        }
  2384	
  2385	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2386	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2387	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2388	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  2389	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2390	        canvas.drawText(
  2391	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  2392	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,
  2393	            baselineY,
  2394	            rememberMeTextPaint
  2395	        )
  2396	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = 255
  2397	    }

  2399	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  2400	        canvas: Canvas,
  2401	        centerX: Float,
  2402	        topY: Float,
  2403	        stageLeft: Float,
  2404	        stageTop: Float,
  2405	        stageScale: Float,
  2406	        alpha: Float
  2407	    ) {
  2408	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  2409	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2410	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  2411	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2412	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2413	
  2414	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (chevronAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2415	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2416	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  2417	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  2418	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2419	    }

  2421	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  2422	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  2423	    }
  2424	
  2425	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(
  2426	        source: Bitmap,
  2427	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean = false
  2428	    ): Bitmap {
  2429	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2430	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2431	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2432	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2433	
  2434	        val mask = createAvatarCharacterMask(pixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2435	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2436	            if (mask[index]) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  2437	        }
  2438	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2439	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2440	        }
  2441	    }
  2442	
  2443	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  2444	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2445	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2446	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2447	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2448	
  2449	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  2450	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  2451	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  2452	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  2453	
  2454	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2455	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  2456	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  2457	            val dx = x - centerX
  2458	            val dy = y - centerY
  2459	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared) {
  2460	                Color.TRANSPARENT
  2461	            } else {
  2462	                pixels[index]
  2463	            }
  2464	        }
  2465	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2466	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2467	        }
  2468	    }
  2469	
  2470	    private fun createAvatarCharacterMask(
  2471	        pixels: IntArray,
  2472	        bitmapWidth: Int,
  2473	        bitmapHeight: Int,
  2474	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2475	    ): BooleanArray {
  2476	        val coloredPixels = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2477	        pixels.forEachIndexed { index, pixel ->
  2478	            coloredPixels[index] = isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2479	        }
  2480	
  2481	        val mask = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2482	        val outlineRadius = 7
  2483	        for (y in 0 until bitmapHeight) {
  2484	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  2485	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  2486	                if (!coloredPixels[index]) continue
  2487	
  2488	                mask[index] = true
  2489	                for (dy in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2490	                    val sampleY = y + dy
  2491	                    if (sampleY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  2492	                    for (dx in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2493	                        val sampleX = x + dx
  2494	                        if (sampleX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  2495	                        val sampleIndex = sampleY * bitmapWidth + sampleX
  2496	                        val samplePixel = pixels[sampleIndex]
  2497	                        val sampleAlpha = Color.alpha(samplePixel)
  2498	                        val sampleMax = maxOf(
  2499	                            Color.red(samplePixel),
  2500	                            Color.green(samplePixel),
  2501	                            Color.blue(samplePixel)
  2502	                        )
  2503	                        if (sampleAlpha > 16 && sampleMax <= 44) {
  2504	                            mask[sampleIndex] = true
  2505	                        }
  2506	                    }
  2507	                }
  2508	            }
  2509	        }
  2510	        return mask
  2511	    }
  2512	
  2513	    private fun isColorfulAvatarPixel(
  2514	        pixel: Int,
  2515	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2516	    ): Boolean {
  2517	        val alpha = Color.alpha(pixel)
  2518	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  2519	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  2520	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  2521	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  2522	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  2523	        if (alpha <= 16) return false
  2524	
  2525	        val chroma = channelMax - channelMin
  2526	        return (channelMax > 48 && chroma > 22) ||
  2527	            (includeDarkSaturatedPixels && channelMax > 36 && chroma > 14)
  2528	    }
  2529	
  2530	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  2531	        return when {
  2532	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  2533	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  2534	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  2535	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  2536	            else -> null
  2537	        }
  2538	    }
  2539	
  2540	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  2541	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  2542	    }

  2544	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
  2545	        return bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis) >= 1f
  2546	    }

  2548	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  2549	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  2550	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  2551	
  2552	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2553	
  2554	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2555	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  2556	            val bounds = target.bounds
  2557	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  2558	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  2559	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  2560	            val dx = x - centerX
  2561	            val dy = y - centerY
  2562	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  2563	        }?.profile
  2564	    }

  2689	    private fun mockCreateAvatarNavHit(x: Float, y: Float): MockAvatarCarouselDirection? {

  2693	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())

  2695	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2696	        val columnX = mockCreateFormX() + (MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMNS - 1) * (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP)
  2697	        val firstFieldY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset + focusShiftY
  2698	        val avatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX, firstFieldY)
  2699	        return MockAvatarCarouselDirection.values().firstOrNull { direction ->
  2700	            mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(x, y, direction, avatarBounds, stage)
  2701	        }
  2702	    }

  2704	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  2705	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return false
  2706	
  2707	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  2708	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2709	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2710	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2711	        val columnX = mockCreateFormX() + (MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMNS - 1) * (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP)
  2712	        val firstFieldY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset + focusShiftY

  2717	        val right = stage.left + (carouselBounds.x + carouselBounds.w) * stage.scale
  2718	        val bottom = stage.top + (carouselBounds.y + carouselBounds.h) * stage.scale

  2722	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2723	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  2724	
  2725	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  2726	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  2727	        if (
  2728	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2729	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  2730	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  2731	        ) {
  2732	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  2733	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  2734	        }
  2735	
  2736	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  2737	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2738	        }
  2739	        return true
  2740	    }

  2742	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  2743	        if (
  2744	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  2745	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  2746	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  2747	        ) {
  2748	            return null
  2749	        }
  2750	
  2751	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  2752	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2753	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2754	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2755	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2756	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2757	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2758	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  2759	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  2760	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  2761	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2762	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  2763	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  2764	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  2765	        }
  2766	
  2767	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  2768	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  2769	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  2770	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  2771	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  2772	        }
  2773	        return null
  2774	    }

  2776	    private fun mockDropdownOptionsHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdown? {
  2777	        if (
  2778	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  2779	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  2780	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  2781	        ) {
  2782	            return null
  2783	        }
  2784	
  2785	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  2786	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2787	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2788	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2789	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2790	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2791	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2792	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  2793	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  2794	        val top = stage.top + yStart * stage.scale
  2795	        val bottom = top + mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown) * mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale
  2796	        return if (pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom) dropdown else null
  2797	    }

  2799	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2800	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  2801	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  2802	
  2803	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  2804	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  2805	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  2806	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  2807	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  2808	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  2809	        }
  2810	
  2811	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  2812	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  2813	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  2814	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2815	        }
  2816	        return true
  2817	    }

  2819	    private fun mockBackChevronHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  2820	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  2821	
  2822	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  2823	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2824	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2825	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2826	        val chevronTopY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  2827	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> {

  2829	                val lastFieldY = mockActiveFieldY(lastField, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2830	                lastFieldY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE +
  2831	                    MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_TOP_GAP
  2832	            }

  2838	        }

  2842	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2843	        if (mockDragReturnInProgress || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  2844	
  2845	        val upwardDragY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  2846	        if (upwardDragY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  2847	
  2848	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  2849	        return upwardDragY > abs(dragX)
  2850	    }
  2851	
  2852	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  2853	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  2854	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  2855	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  2856	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  2857	    }

  2962	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselBounds(
  2963	        columnX: Float,
  2964	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2965	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  2966	    ): Bounds {
  2967	        val centerX = columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  2968	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  2969	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  2970	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  2971	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  2972	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) * 2f
  2973	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  2974	        return Bounds(
  2975	            centerX - side / 2f,
  2976	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  2977	            side,
  2978	            side
  2979	        )
  2980	    }

  3001	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(
  3002	        pointerX: Float,
  3003	        pointerY: Float,
  3004	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  3005	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  3006	        stage: StageMetrics
  3007	    ): Boolean {
  3008	        val centerX = stage.left + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  3009	        val centerY = stage.top + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  3010	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3011	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  3012	        return pointerX in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  3013	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  3014	    }

  3016	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  3017	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  3018	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  3019	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  3020	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3021	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3022	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  3023	            activeComposingText = ""
  3024	            focusedMockField = field
  3025	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  3026	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  3027	        }
  3028	        requestFocus()
  3029	        post {
  3030	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3031	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  3032	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  3033	        }
  3034	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3035	    }

  3140	    private fun mockActiveFieldX(field: MockSignInField): Float {
  3141	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) return mockForgotPasswordFormX()
  3142	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return mockFormX()
  3143	
  3144	        val middleColumnX = mockCreateFormX() + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP
  3145	        return when (field) {
  3146	            MockSignInField.Username,
  3147	            MockSignInField.Password,
  3148	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> mockCreateFormX()
  3149	            MockSignInField.Email -> middleColumnX
  3150	            MockSignInField.Month -> middleColumnX
  3151	            MockSignInField.Day -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP
  3152	            MockSignInField.Year -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DAY_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP * 2f
  3153	        }

  3156	    private fun mockActiveFieldY(field: MockSignInField, yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  3157	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  3158	            return mockForgotPasswordFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3159	        }
  3160	
  3161	        val rowIndex = if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  3162	            when (field) {
  3163	                MockSignInField.Username,
  3164	                MockSignInField.Email -> 0
  3165	                MockSignInField.Password -> 1
  3166	                MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword,
  3167	                MockSignInField.Month,
  3168	                MockSignInField.Day,
  3169	                MockSignInField.Year -> 2
  3170	            }
  3171	        } else {
  3172	            activeMockFields().indexOf(field).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3173	        }
  3174	        return MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset + focusShiftY + rowIndex * (MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP)
  3175	    }

  3278	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3279	        val optionCount = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size

  3281	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT

  3289	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3290	    }

  3292	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3293	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  3294	    }
  3295	
  3296	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3297	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3298	    }

  3301	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  3302	    }

  3304	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  3305	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  3306	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  3307	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  3308	    }

  3310	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  3311	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3312	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  3313	            return
  3314	        }
  3315	
  3316	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  3317	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  3318	    }

  3402	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  3403	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  3404	            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3405	            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3406	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()

  3449	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
  3450	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()

  3452	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()

  3455	    private fun mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress(): Float {
  3456	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return if (
  3457	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3458	        ) {
  3459	            1f
  3460	        } else {
  3461	            0f
  3462	        }
  3463	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3464	        return (elapsed / MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3465	    }

  3469	    }

  3471	    private fun finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded() {
  3472	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return
  3473	        if (forgotPasswordSubmissionState != MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  3474	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3475	            return
  3476	        }
  3477	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS) return
  3478	
  3479	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3480	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3481	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3482	    }

  3486	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle

  3491	    private fun startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  3492	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  3493	        startProgress: Float = 0f
  3494	    ) {
  3495	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount || isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) return
  3496	
  3497	        val nextIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(direction)
  3498	        if (nextIndex == mockCreateAvatarIndex) return
  3499	
  3500	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3501	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
  3502	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = nextIndex
  3503	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = direction
  3504	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = startProgress.coerceIn(0f, 0.96f)
  3505	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = nextIndex
  3506	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  3507	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3508	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3509	    }
  3510	
  3511	    private fun mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection): Int {
  3512	        return (mockCreateAvatarIndex + direction.indexDelta + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT) % MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT
  3513	    }

  3517	            mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX < 0f -> MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  3518	            mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX > 0f -> MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  3519	            else -> null
  3520	        }
  3521	    }

  3525	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  3526	        return mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize), stage.scale)
  3527	    }

  3530	        val slideDistancePx = (slideDistance * stageScale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  3531	        return (abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) / slideDistancePx).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3532	    }

  3543	        val startMillis = mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis ?: return 1f
  3544	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3545	        val elapsedProgress = (elapsed / MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CAROUSEL_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3546	        return lerpFloat(mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress, 1f, elapsedProgress).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3547	    }

  3576	    private fun updateMockFormLabelAnimation() {
  3577	        val now = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  3578	        val elapsed = (now - lastFormLabelAnimationMillis).coerceIn(0L, 48L)
  3579	        lastFormLabelAnimationMillis = now
  3580	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()
  3581	        mockFieldLabelFloatProgress.keys.forEach { field ->
  3582	            mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] = moveToward(
  3583	                mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] ?: 0f,
  3584	                mockSignInFieldLabelTarget(field),
  3585	                step
  3586	            )
  3587	        }
  3588	    }

  3590	    private fun updateMockFormFocusAnimation() {

  3593	        lastFormFocusAnimationMillis = now
  3594	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_FORM_FOCUS_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat()
  3595	        mockFormFocusProgress = moveToward(mockFormFocusProgress, mockFormFocusTarget(), step)
  3596	    }

  3598	    private fun updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation() {
  3599	        val now = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  3600	        val elapsed = (now - lastMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimationMillis).coerceIn(0L, 48L)
  3601	        lastMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimationMillis = now
  3602	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_LANDSCAPE_INPUT_LIFT_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat()
  3603	        mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress = moveToward(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress, mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(), step)
  3604	    }

  3606	    private fun updateMockInputDimAnimation() {
  3607	        val target = mockInputDimTarget()
  3608	        if (target > 0f) {
  3609	            mockInputDimActiveDropdown = expandedMockDropdown
  3610	            mockInputDimActiveField = if (expandedMockDropdown == null) focusedMockField else null
  3611	        }
  3612	
  3613	        val now = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  3614	        val elapsed = (now - lastMockInputDimAnimationMillis).coerceIn(0L, 48L)
  3615	        lastMockInputDimAnimationMillis = now
  3616	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_INPUT_DIM_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat()
  3617	        mockInputDimProgress = moveToward(mockInputDimProgress, target, step)
  3618	
  3619	        if (mockInputDimProgress == 0f && target == 0f) {
  3620	            mockInputDimActiveField = null
  3621	            mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  3622	        }
  3623	    }
  3624	
  3625	    private fun isMockFormLabelAnimating(): Boolean {
  3626	        return mockFieldLabelFloatProgress.any { (field, progress) ->
  3627	            progress != mockSignInFieldLabelTarget(field)
  3628	        }
  3629	    }

  3631	    private fun isMockFormFocusAnimating(): Boolean {
  3632	        return mockFormFocusProgress != mockFormFocusTarget()
  3633	    }

  3635	    private fun isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating(): Boolean {

  3637	    }

  3639	    private fun isMockInputDimAnimating(): Boolean {
  3640	        return mockInputDimProgress != mockInputDimTarget()
  3641	    }

  3645	    }

  3659	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  3660	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  3661	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  3662	        return if (
  3663	            focusedMockField != null ||
  3664	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  3665	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  3666	        ) {
  3667	            1f
  3668	        } else {
  3669	            0f
  3670	        }
  3671	    }
  3672	
  3673	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  3674	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  3675	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  3676	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  3677	    }
  3678	
  3679	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  3680	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  3681	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  3682	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  3683	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  3684	        }
  3685	
  3686	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  3687	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  3688	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  3689	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  3690	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  3691	    }
  3692	
  3693	    private fun mockInputDimTarget(): Float {
  3694	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  3695	        if (expandedMockDropdown != null) return 1f
  3696	        val awaitingField = mockInputDimAwaitingField ?: return 0f
  3697	        return if (focusedMockField == awaitingField) 1f else 0f
  3698	    }

  3706	        return if (
  3707	            focusedMockField != null ||
  3708	            activeMockFields().any { mockSignInFieldText(it).isNotEmpty() }

  3778	    private fun setMockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField, text: String) {
  3779	        val limitedText = text.take(MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS)
  3780	        when (field) {
  3781	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText = limitedText
  3782	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
  3783	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText = limitedText
  3784	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  3785	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText
  3786	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText = limitedText
  3787	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText = limitedText
  3788	        }
  3789	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword && field == MockSignInField.Email) {
  3790	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  3791	        }
  3792	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3793	        if (mockInputDimAwaitingField == field) {
  3794	            mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  3795	        }
  3796	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  3797	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  3798	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3799	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3800	    }
  3801	
  3802	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  3803	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3804	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  3805	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  3806	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3807	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  3808	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3809	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  3810	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  3811	        activeComposingText = ""
  3812	        return true
  3813	    }
  3814	
  3815	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  3816	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3817	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  3818	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3819	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  3820	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3821	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  3822	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  3823	        activeComposingText = composingText
  3824	        return true
  3825	    }
  3826	
  3827	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  3828	        activeComposingText = ""
  3829	        return true
  3830	    }
  3831	
  3832	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  3833	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3834	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3835	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  3836	            activeComposingText = ""
  3837	            return true
  3838	        }
  3839	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  3840	        activeComposingText = ""
  3841	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  3842	        return true
  3843	    }
  3844	
  3845	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  3846	        activeComposingText = ""
  3847	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  3848	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  3849	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  3850	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  3851	        } else if (
  3852	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  3853	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  3854	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  3855	        ) {
  3856	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  3857	        } else {
  3858	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3859	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  3860	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3861	        }
  3862	        return true
  3863	    }
  3864	
  3865	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  3866	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  3867	        return when (keyCode) {
  3868	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  3869	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  3870	            else -> {
  3871	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  3872	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  3873	            }
  3874	        }
  3875	    }
  3876	
  3877	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  3878	        return buildString {
  3879	            input.forEach { character ->
  3880	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  3881	                    append(character)
  3882	                }
  3883	            }
  3884	        }
  3885	    }
  3886	
  3887	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  3888	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  3889	
  3890	        var startIndex = 0
  3891	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  3892	            startIndex += 1
  3893	        }
  3894	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  3895	    }
  3896	
  3897	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  3898	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  3899	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  3900	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  3901	                lines += ""
  3902	            }
  3903	
  3904	            var currentLine = ""
  3905	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  3906	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  3907	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  3908	                .forEach { word ->
  3909	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  3910	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  3911	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  3912	                    } else {
  3913	                        lines += currentLine
  3914	                        currentLine = word
  3915	                    }
  3916	                }
  3917	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  3918	                lines += currentLine
  3919	            }
  3920	        }
  3921	        return lines
  3922	    }
  3923	
  3924	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  3925	        return when {
  3926	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  3927	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  3928	            else -> current
  3929	        }
  3930	    }

  3933	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null

  3935	        val stageScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  3936	        return StageMetrics(
  3937	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  3938	            top = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f + mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  3939	            scale = stageScale
  3940	        )
  3941	    }

  3995	        onIntroSnapshotChanged?.invoke(snapshot)

  4079	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  4080	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  4081	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  4082	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4083	        return lerpFloat(
  4084	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  4085	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  4086	            progress
  4087	        )
  4088	    }
  4089	
  4090	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  4091	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  4092	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  4093	    }
  4094	
  4095	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  4096	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  4097	    }
  4098	
  4099	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  4100	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  4101	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  4102	            .roundToInt()
  4103	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  4104	            .toLong()
  4105	    }
  4106	
  4107	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  4108	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  4109	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  4110	
  4111	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  4112	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  4113	            activeMockFlow = null
  4114	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  4115	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  4116	            pressedMockField = null
  4117	            pressedRememberMe = false
  4118	            pressedBackChevron = false
  4119	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4120	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  4121	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4122	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  4123	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  4124	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  4125	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  4126	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  4127	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4128	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  4129	            notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4130	            return
  4131	        }
  4132	
  4133	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  4134	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  4135	    }

  4137	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  4138	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  4139	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  4140	        }
  4141	
  4142	        override fun setComposingText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  4143	            return replaceMockSignInComposingText(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  4144	        }
  4145	
  4146	        override fun finishComposingText(): Boolean {
  4147	            return finishMockSignInComposingText()
  4148	        }
  4149	
  4150	        override fun deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  4151	            return deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength)
  4152	        }
  4153	
  4154	        override fun deleteSurroundingTextInCodePoints(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  4155	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
  4156	        }
  4157	
  4158	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4159	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()
  4160	            return text.takeLast(n.coerceAtLeast(0))
  4161	        }
  4162	
  4163	        override fun getTextAfterCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4164	            return ""
  4165	        }
  4166	
  4167	        override fun getSelectedText(flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4168	            return ""
  4169	        }
  4170	
  4171	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  4172	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  4173	        }
  4174	
  4175	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4176	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4177	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  4178	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4179	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4180	                else -> {
  4181	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4182	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  4183	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  4184	                    } else {
  4185	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  4186	                    }
  4187	                }
  4188	            }
  4189	        }
  4190	    }
  4191	}
  4192

  4340	    val x: Float,
  4341	    val y: Float,
  4342	    val w: Float,
  4343	    val h: Float
  4344	)
  4345	
  4346	private inline fun <reified T : Enum<T>> enumValueOrNull(name: String): T? {
  4347	    return runCatching { enumValueOf<T>(name) }.getOrNull()
  4348	}
  4349	
  4350	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  4351	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  4352	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4353	}
  4354	
  4355	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4356	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  4357	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4358	}
  4359	
  4360	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4361	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4362	}
  4363	
  4364	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4365	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4366	}
  4367	
  4368	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  4369	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4370	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  4371	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  4372	    return base + bounce
  4373	}
  4374	
  4375	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  4376	
  4377	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  4378	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  4379	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  4380	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  4381	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  4382	)
  4383	
  4384	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  4385	    x = bounds.x,
  4386	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  4387	    w = bounds.w,
  4388	    h = bounds.h
  4389	)
  4390	
  4391	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4392	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4393	    return Color.argb(
  4394	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  4395	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  4396	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  4397	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  4398	    )
  4399	}
  4400	
  4401	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4402	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  4403	}
  4404	
  4405	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4406	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4407	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4408	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4409	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4410	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4411	    return output
  4412	}
  4413	
  4414	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4415	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4416	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4417	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  4418	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  4419	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4420	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  4421	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  4422	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  4423	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  4424	    return output
  4425	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    32	
    33	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    34	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    35	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

     4	}

============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \
   150	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml" \
   151	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml"
   152	  do
   153	    print_file "$file"
   154	  done
   155	
   156	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   157	
   158	
   159	============================================================
   160	FILE CONTENTS — ROOT / TOOLING
   161	============================================================
   162	
   163	EOF
   164	
   165	  for file in \
   166	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   167	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/copilot-instructions.md" \
   168	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/mcp.json" \
   169	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/tasks.json" \
   170	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   171	    "$PROJECT_DIR/build.gradle.kts" \
   172	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build.gradle.kts" \
   173	    "$PROJECT_DIR/settings.gradle.kts" \
   174	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle.properties" \
   175	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties" \
   176	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   177	  do
   178	    print_file "$file"
   179	  done
   180	
   181	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — DRAWABLE-NODPI" "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi"
   182	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / SCREENSHOT INVENTORY" "$PROJECT_DIR/screenshots"
   183	
   184	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   185	
   186	
   187	============================================================
   188	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   189	============================================================
   190	
   191	The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
   192	- .git
   193	- .gradle
   194	- .idea
   195	- app/build
   196	- build
   197	- .kotlin
   198	- .tmp
   199	- .cache
   200	- .DS_Store
   201	- local.properties
   202	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   203	- *.jks
   204	- *.keystore
   205	
   206	The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
   207	
   208	EOF
   209	}
   210	
   211	write_snapshot
   212	
   213	echo "Done."
   214	echo ""
   215	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   216	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   217	echo ""
   218	echo "Open it with:"
   219	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
